&lt;/project_context>
**任务**: 补跑 J=16 Cross-Family IRT 剩余 5 个 domain 的 θ 提取

**服务器**: cqa1-21977
**项目路径**: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp006
**环境**: source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate

**背景**:
- J=16 IRT rerun (--skip-pooled) 已为 3 个 domain 保存了 theta: STEM_Math, STEM_Science, STEM_CS_Eng
- 剩余 5 个 domain 缺 theta: Medical, Humanities, Social_Sciences, Business_Law, Other
- 之前 PID 4670 已退出（pooled 崩溃导致整个脚本终止）
- 需要重跑这 5 个 domain（带 theta saving，跳过 pooled）

**约束**:
- **不要跑 pooled fit** — Director 明确禁止
- **不要重跑已完成的 3 个 domain** — 浪费时间
- 容器对长时间运行进程有安全限制（~88 分钟后 kill），每个 domain ~6-14 分钟应该安全

**步骤**:

1. 查看脚本结构，确认如何只跑特定 domain:
   ```bash
   grep -n "domains\|domain_list\|DOMAINS\|for.*domain\|skip" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp006/run_mmlu_domain_gamma_j16_cross_family.py | head -30
   ```

2. 检查哪些 domain 已有 theta（确认现状）:
   ```bash
   for d in STEM_Math STEM_Science STEM_CS_Eng Medical Humanities Social_Sciences Business_Law Other; do
     echo -n "$d: "
     python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp006/mmlu_domain_irt_j16_crossfamily/$d/irt_results.json')); print('has theta' if 'theta_per_model' in d or 'theta' in d else 'NO theta')" 2>/dev/null || echo "file missing"
   done
   ```

3. 修改脚本或写新脚本，只跑缺 theta 的 5 个 domain:
   - 选项 A: 如果脚本支持 `--domains` 参数，直接指定
   - 选项 B: 写一个小 wrapper 只循环 5 个缺失的 domain
   - 选项 C: 修改脚本添加 "skip if theta already exists" 逻辑
   
4. 确保 theta 保存逻辑正确:
   - 查看已成功的 3 个 domain 的 theta 是怎么保存的
   - 确认保存格式（theta_per_model: list of posterior means, 顺序对应 model list）

5. 在 tmux 中运行:
   ```bash
   tmux new-session -d -s j16_theta_5domains "source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && cd /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp006 && &lt;command> 2>&1 | tee /root/autodl-tmp/exp006/j16_theta_5domains.log"
   ```
   - 预计 5 × 10min = ~50 min

6. 等待完成后，验证所有 8 domains 都有 theta:
   ```bash
   for d in Medical Humanities Social_Sciences Business_Law Other; do
     echo -n "$d: "
     python3 -c "import json; d=json.load(open('/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp006/mmlu_domain_irt_j16_crossfamily/$d/irt_results.json')); print('theta OK' if 'theta_per_model' in d or 'theta' in d else 'NO theta')" 2>/dev/null || echo "FAILED"
   done
   ```

7. 完成后汇报完整 θ per model 表（所有 8 domains × 16 models）

**汇报**: 
- 完整 θ 表（8 domains）
- Qwen-3B vs Llama-3B θ 差异表（所有 domains，d005 和 d025 两个共有 dosage）
- 是否有 domain 差异模式（某些 domain Δ更大？）

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Build domain indices\\n    subject_list = build_subject_index_map(args.mmlu_arrow)\\n    domain_indices = build_domain_col_indices(subject_list, n_total_items)\\n\\n    domains_to_fit = args.domains if args.domains else list(DOMAIN_MAP.keys())\\n\\n    if args.dry_run:\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n=== DRY RUN: N_models={len(models)}, N_items={n_total_items} ===\\\\n\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"{'Domain':<18} {'N_items':>7} {'Mean_acc':>8}\\\")\\n        print(\\\"-\\\" * 40)\\n        for domain in domains_to_fit:\\n            idx = domain_indices[domain]\\n            if not idx:\\n                print(f\\\"{domain:<18} {'0':>7} {'N/A':>8}\\\")\\n                continue\\n            acc = full_responses[:, idx].mean()\\n            print(f\\\"{domain:<18} {len(idx):>7} {acc:>8.4f}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\nModels ({len(models)}):\\\")\\n        for m in models:\\n            print(f\\\"  {m['dirname']} (scale={m.get('scale')}, d={m['dosage']})\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\nDosages: {dosages.tolist()}\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    # Fit per-domain\\n    args_dict = {\\n        \\\"chains\\\": args.chains, \\\"warmup\\\": args.warmup,\\n        \\\"samples\\\": args.samples, \\\"adapt_delta\\\": args.adapt_delta,\\n        \\\"max_treedepth\\\": args.max_treedepth, \\\"seed\\\": args.seed,\\n    }\\n\\n    all_results = {}\\n\\n    if args.pooled_only:\\n        for domain in list(DOMAIN_MAP.keys()):\\n            result_path = output_dir / domain / \\\"irt_results.json\\\"\\n            if result_path.exists():\\n                with open(result_path) as f:\\n                    all_results[domain] = json.load(f)\\n                log.info(\\\"Loaded existing result for %s\\\", domain)\\n        domains_to_fit = []\\n\\n    for domain in domains_to_fit:\\n        idx = domain_indices[domain]\\n        if len(idx) < 10:\\n            log.warning(\\\"Skipping %s: only %d items\\\", domain, len(idx))\\n            continue\\n\\n        log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n        log.info(\\\"Fitting domain: %s (%d items)\\\", domain, len(idx))\\n\\n        domain_resp = full_responses[:, idx]\\n        domain_exp = rrf_exposure[:, idx]\\n\\n        result = fit_domain(domain, domain_resp, domain_exp, args_dict, output_dir)\\n        all_results[domain] = result\\n\\n        g = result[\\\"gamma\\\"]\\n        d = result[\\\"diagnostics\\\"]\\n        log.info(\\\"[%s] gamma=%.4f [%.4f, %.4f], rhat=%.4f, ESS=%.0f, div=%d, time=%.0fs\\\",\\n                 domain, g[\\\"mean\\\"], g[\\\"q025\\\"], g[\\\"q975\\\"],\\n                 d.get(\\\"max_rhat\\\", 0), d.get(\\\"min_ess\\\", 0),\\n                 d.get(\\\"divergent_count\\\", 0), result[\\\"fit_time_sec\\\"])\\n\\n    # Pooled fit\\n    if not args.skip_pooled or args.pooled_only:\\n        log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 50)\\n        log.info(\\\"Fitting POOLED model (all domains combined)\\\")\\n        pooled_result = fit_pooled(full_responses, rrf_exposure, args_dict, output_dir)\\n        all_results[\\\"pooled\\\"] = pooled_result\\n        pg = pooled_result[\\\"gamma\\\"]\\n        log.info(\\\"[pooled] gamma=%.4f [%.4f, %.4f], PPP=%.4f\\\",\\n                 pg[\\\"mean\\\"], pg[\\\"q025\\\"], pg[\\\"q975\\\"], _ppp_float(pooled_result[\\\"ppp\\\"]))\\n\\n    if all_results:\\n        print(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 70)\\n        generate_summary(all_results, output_dir, len(models))\\n        with open(output_dir / \\\"all_domain_results.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)\\n        log.info(\\\"Done. Results in %s\\\", output_dir)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.98}}"
